Ground Truth report library

Can Luck Match a Trading Champion's Record?

1. Question and conditional answer

Full test report. The year cannot tell skill from chance under the assumed annual recipe. The completed record is rare at this test’s precision in the headline model, unless a designated replacement takes much larger persistent risks in the separately funded, zero-cost, no-floor scenario described below. This is a conditional model answer, not a reconstruction of the contest.

Arm A headline: assumed 300 people, payoff 0.5 times risk, one trade/active day, 150 days/year, cost 0.02R per trade, $1,500 absolute floor, one separately funded $5,000 account/person/event; shared overlapping histories; ANY identity matching the fixed joint record. Fixed 3% risk (F03) and guessed persistent risk mixtures FU/FH are separate components; no pooling. Real entrants/accounts unknown. In the post-results 72-row subset, 29 qualify for the model envelope and 43 do not. The broad bundle alone does not imply the qualifying range.

REAL-WORLD CALIBRATION UNKNOWN — entrants/accounts; OPPORTUNITY UNCALIBRATED; actual-contest skill verdict unavailable. No actual-contest skill verdict. Payoff, activity, costs and historical enforcement remain unknown.

2. Attributed year versus completed record

On World Class Edge, Patrick Nill said he made 300% in the championship that year. This is an attributed ongoing-year self-report, not an independently audited return. The normalized quote comes from 27.039–34.399 seconds; source audio is not used in the film.

The later organizer snapshot reports 305.1% through October 9, 2026. It cannot date the interview, and an ongoing lead is not a completed win.

Organizer-reported completed Forex event seasons: World 2021-2025 ranks 2,2,2,4,2; Global 2021-2022 through 2025-2026 ranks 1,1,2,3,3. Nine podiums in ten event seasons, four of five World and five of five Global including two wins; overlapping windows, not ten independent years. Excludes duplicate Forex listing, contested Futures podium, other Futures placements and ongoing seasons.

SeriesSeasonsPublished ranksPodiumsWins used in threshold
World Forex2021–20252, 2, 2, 4, 24 of 5Not a World-win requirement
Global Forex2021–2022 to 2025–20261, 1, 2, 3, 35 of 52 of 5

These ten event windows span overlapping calendars across 2021–2026. A podium means a top-three finish. The test uses the same identity across both series: at least four World podiums in five seasons AND five Global podiums in five seasons including at least two wins. It does not require the exact chronological rank sequence, and a different winner each year cannot assemble the record.

Summary of the plan locked before the run. Summary, not the full plan text; private lock is not a public timestamp proof. Main terms and selected report diagnostics; not the complete private registry or raw results.

Private lock 3aa19e6 fixed the main terms before the run, after selecting the historical target. The pre-run launch addendum c2a0e85 and post-results Amendment 1 are labelled separately below.

4. Sources, assumptions and two arms

For the designated replacement in the 72-row post-results subset: persistent 2% or 3% risk versus 0.2% opponents; 150 active days/year; one or two trades/day; guessed payoff 0.5, 1 or 2 times risk; independent account strategies; three separately funded $5,000 entries per person, fresh each event after ruin; zero trading costs and no loss floor. Assumed field sizes and overlap choices vary; real-world calibration is unknown.

Arm A is a parametric model: its no-edge win chance is 1/(1+b) before trading costs. Arm B draws fair long/short directions on saved SPY and QQQ intraday prices from 2012–2025. It uses b=.5, stop fraction .002, persistent 2%/3% or FU/FH risks, both activity rates, the three field sizes, P/JOINT and three bar-fill conventions. Those fills are sensitivities, not a confidence set. This narrower Arm B excludes wider payoff/stop/cost/rule/private/high-risk robustness checks.

Two current Forex rule documents require $5,000 per separate entry; multiple accounts permitted, best qualifying account ranks. Permission is not actual use or historical enforcement; not summed household returns. World extract retains source joining error "isfunded".

The source rule pages were retrieved October 13, 2026. Current entry permission does not establish historical enforcement, real entrant counts, actual account use or funding after ruin. No personal funding ledger accompanies this test.

5. Headline components and precision

Annual recipe: N300, f.03, b.5, d1, H104, cost .02R, $1,500 floor, K1, endpoint +300%. NON-DISCRIMINATING UNDER THIS RECIPE; both single-account +.10R/+.20R alternatives have tiny endpoint probabilities. Corrected null 2.0289416069918675e-13; retired print 1.99840144433e-13. No personal skill probability.

Arm A headline: assumed 300 people, payoff 0.5 times risk, one trade/active day, 150 days/year, cost 0.02R per trade, $1,500 absolute floor, one separately funded $5,000 account/person/event; shared overlapping histories; ANY identity matching the fixed joint record. Fixed 3% risk (F03) and guessed persistent risk mixtures FU/FH are separate components; no pooling. Real entrants/accounts unknown.

ComponentMatches / fieldsOrdinary 95% intervalSimultaneous 95% intervalLabel
F030 / 1640[0, 0.00224678892306][0, 0.008829636863]RARE UNDER THIS REGISTERED MODEL; calibration unknown; arms unresolved
FU0 / 1640[0, 0.00224678892306][0, 0.008829636863]RARE UNDER THIS REGISTERED MODEL; calibration unknown; arms unresolved
FH0 / 1640[0, 0.00224678892306][0, 0.008829636863]RARE UNDER THIS REGISTERED MODEL; calibration unknown; arms unresolved

The three component samples are not pooled. The aggregate takes the weakest evidence of rarity: rare requires three rare labels; one envelope label would make it envelope; otherwise it is unresolved. Zero observations are not zero probability. With the registered interval family, one observed match in a component would put its upper bound around .0106, above the .01 rare threshold.

578 of 770 same-N300 ANY/J variants agree with headline; correlated configurations, not independent confirmations. Excludes other N, sentinel S02/S03, W-only/G-only, non-Q2; invalid/outside-support/non-discriminating excluded before denominator (0 here). Real entrants/accounts unknown.

Report-only presentation of a registered question omitted from the film: one account, f.03, b.5, d1, cost .02R, $1,500 floor, 150 active days, +300% endpoint at least half the time. Registered grid bracket p=[.88,1], width .12; cost break-even p=.68; net edge [.30,.48] R/trade. q(.88)=.363587588044 and q(1)=1. This is a sufficient grid endpoint, not an exact minimum or sizing advice. Fixed grid has 42 points (step .005 from break-even, then forced 1); the last gap is retained.

6. Full 72-row membership table and all film figures

Post-results subset of registered cells: Arm A F002 JOINT designated-replacement J rows, N100/300/1000, b0.5/1/2, d1/2, D0/D1, S02/S03. Excludes ANY, W-only/G-only, other risks/configurations, Q1/Q1_ALT/Q3, Arm B and report-only diagnostics. The 29 qualifying rows are selected by their envelope label; the 43 others are CANNOT TELL.

Among those 72 conditional model rows, the 29 qualifying point estimates run from 228/1640 to 446/1640: rounded reciprocals about one in seven to one in four. The 43 other rows go down to 67/1640, about one in twenty-four; their CANNOT TELL labels do not prove rarity. This is a range across settings, separate from the two interval columns. The full joint combination, not separate axis totals, determines membership.

7. Single-change scan, pairs unmeasured and row flags

0 of 3,456 tested single-change J rows reach envelope (864 per C0/C05/L0/K3). Pairs unmeasured; no inference that the whole bundle is necessary. Excludes P, JOINT, W/G-only, non-Q2 and Arm B.

The membership CSV retains each of the 72 source flag strings in full. Flags vary by row: an unresolved dependence, activity, payoff or JOINT comparison is not a demonstrated sensitivity. JOINT-SENSITIVE does not isolate cost, floor and account effects. The common ANY-search phrase in the source flags describes ANY elsewhere; S02/S03 rows measure the designated replacement.

At N300, b.5, d1, D1, with a 3% replacement versus 0.2% opponents, primary rules yield 0/1640; removing cost alone 12/1640; removing the floor alone 0/1640; three accounts alone 92/1640; JOINT 443/1640. These five matched configurations differ only as named. No two-change combinations were measured, so the contrast does not identify a minimal sufficient bundle.

8. Limits, data gap and Amendment 1

Registered 218,202 cells: A Q1 62,214; A Q1_ALT 101,076; A Q2 15,558; A Q3 3,066; B Q1 31,104; B Q2 5,184. This report summarizes main questions, primary results, precision, scope and deficiencies; its result ledger prints the 72-row film subset, not the entire raw registry. Report-only alternative targets, historical slices, analytical comparisons, other per-cell diagnostics and raw alternative rows are not reproduced cell by cell. The two unretained diagnostic series remain unavailable.

Fewer-than-three-qualifying-people counts and total terminal equity were not retained. They are unavailable, not zero. Recorded funding, losses and maximum qualifying equity cannot reconstruct them without replay; these requested descriptive clauses remain unfulfilled.

DATA-GAP REVIEWED - ACCEPTED WITH DISCLOSURE (29 of 3,520 sessions excluded, incl. the four March-2020 halt days). The halt attribution comes from external historical context recorded in the pre-run addendum; the calendar audit measured missing minutes, not their causes. It measured 14 missing regular minutes per symbol on each of those four dates and 174 missing QQQ minutes on August 22, 2013, attributed separately to a market-data outage. No cause was inferred for the remaining 24 excluded sessions.

SPY/QQQ 2012-2025 liquid-index intraday proxy, not oil/DAX or multiday/weekend reconstruction; 320 fields, simultaneous zero-hit upper .044435345167, too coarse for 1% rare band. Precision failure is not refutation. DATA-GAP REVIEWED - ACCEPTED WITH DISCLOSURE (29 of 3,520 sessions excluded, incl. the four March-2020 halt days). Complete regular minutes required for both SPY/QQQ; 32 of 32 early-close sessions retained. One of 14 years and seven of 168 months tripped the gap criterion; launch addendum accepted disclosure without relaxing eligibility. Broader B payoff, stop, cost, enforcement, private/high-risk grids were excluded before lock. Fair directions do not guarantee zero expected bracket return.

Post-results correction for the primary annual N300 field at f.03/b.5/d1/P/H104/R300/END. Corrected null 2.0289416069918675e-13 replaces retired print 1.99840144433e-13 in explanation. NON-DISCRIMINATING label unchanged. Independent exact-rational and stable -expm1(N*log1p(-q)) probe; analytic error bound, not a row-by-row rerun. Original result receipt preserved.

“Full test report” means a report of the test’s questions, main terms, answers, limitations and the film’s evidence, not a claim to publish the full private plan or every raw output. The downloadable ledger is the complete 72-row film subset only.

9. Reopen criteria and free downloads

Reopen conditionEvidence needed
Supported and discriminating annual recipeEvidence-grounded payoff, activity, costs, horizon and a recipe that distinguishes plausible positive-edge alternatives.
Dated entrants, accounts and fundingRoster and account/funding history by event, including accounts lost and re-entered; not current rules alone.
Prospective record testChoose target and method before observing the next record; the retrospective test cannot establish discovery significance.
Resolution and appropriate instrumentsAdequate fixed repetitions and relevant instrument/exposure data, with a new declared plan; no selective extra draws.
Reproducible source or instrument correctionPreserve original receipts, show the correction and its effect on labels; Amendment 1 is labelled post-results.

Free report linked in description. The membership CSV preserves 72 IDs, their parameters, counts, intervals, labels and row flags; the film CSV preserves placement words and provenance; the JSON summarizes main design terms and labelled changes; the sources CSV identifies saved records and available public references. The downloads do not contain the entire raw study or the private full plan.
